## 3. Top 5 Culturally Sensitive / Nuanced Strings Flagged for Native Review

These 5 strings carry deep philosophical, spiritual, or commercial weight and have been translated with high care. Reviewers should confirm the tone aligns with local spiritual and yoga communities:

| # | Focus Area | English Source | Target Translation | Back-Translation | Linguistic Review Rationale |
| :-: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1** | **1-Year Post-Graduation Mentorship Promise** (`home.whyChoose.postTtcDesc`) | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | De meeste scholen zwaaien studenten uit op de diploma-uitreiking. Wij niet. Gedurende 12 maanden na je opleiding krijg je maandelijkse live sessies met ons: feedback op je lessen als je lesgeeft, begeleiding voor je eigen sadhana als je niet lesgeeft, en een docent aan wie je al je vragen kunt stellen. Dit is geen marketingtruc: studenten uit eerdere lichtingen van onze oprichter oefenen na 3, 4 of zelfs 5 jaar nog steeds online met hem. We hebben simpelweg een belofte gemaakt van wat altijd al vanzelfsprekend was. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Expresses the school's core 12-month mentorship commitment with monthly live video guidance and personal feedback. |
| **2** | **Student Manifesto / Pure Practice Nuance** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoQuote`) | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | «Ik wil eigenlijk niet lesgeven. Ik wil gewoon de diepte in.» | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | High philosophical nuance distinguishing commercial fitness certification from traditional authentic sadhana transmission. |
| **3** | **Philosophy of Self-Discovery (Non-Career Focus)** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoIntro`) | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Meer dan de helft van de mensen die ons schrijven zegt dit, bijna verontschuldigend. Verontschuldig je alsjeblieft niet. In India is yoga nooit een beroepsopleiding geweest; het was een pad van zelfonderzoek. De student stond altijd centraal. | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Reframing yoga teacher training as personal self-realization rather than a commercial career course. |
| **4** | **Sacred Vedic Fire Ceremony (Agni Hotra)** (`course200.inclusions.incCeremonies`) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Traditionele openings- en afsluitende vuurceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Preservation of ancient Vedic sacred fire purification rituals and spiritual tradition. |
| **5** | **Sacred Waterfall Cleansing Ceremony (Melukat)** (`course200.excursionsList[2].description`) | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Maak een tocht naar een heilige waterval voor een reinigingsritueel met zuiver bronwater. | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Balinese Hindu purification ritual at a sacred holy water temple; requires deep cultural respect and sacred phrasing. |

| Homepage: Why YogaGarhi Pillars | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | De meeste scholen zwaaien studenten uit op de diploma-uitreiking. Wij niet. Gedurende 12 maanden na je opleiding krijg je maandelijkse live sessies met ons: feedback op je lessen als je lesgeeft, begeleiding voor je eigen sadhana als je niet lesgeeft, en een docent aan wie je al je vragen kunt stellen. Dit is geen marketingtruc: studenten uit eerdere lichtingen van onze oprichter oefenen na 3, 4 of zelfs 5 jaar nog steeds online met hem. We hebben simpelweg een belofte gemaakt van wat altijd al vanzelfsprekend was. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. | 200hr Bali: overview | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Onze 200 uur yogadocentenopleiding op Bali is een grondig basisprogramma voor wie zelfverzekerd yogales wil geven of yoga diep wil integreren in het eigen leven. YogaGarhi bevindt zich in Gianyar, op circa 15 km van Ubud, te midden van rustgevende natuur. Tijdens de opleiding beoefen je dagelijks asana's, verdiep je je in uitlijning en bestudeer je pranayama, meditatie, filosofie en didactiek. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within NL grammatical structure. |

| Why Choose YogaGarhi | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? This preparatory program is designed specifically for practitioners who want to test their readiness. While most schools start on day one, we begin weeks earlier — online and completely free — helping you build a solid foundation so you arrive confident, grounded, and stress-free. | Twijfel je of je echt klaar bent voor een yogadocentenopleiding (TTC)? Dit voorbereidingsprogramma is speciaal gemaakt om je voorbereiding te testen. Waar de meeste scholen pas op dag één starten, beginnen wij weken eerder, online en helemaal gratis, zodat je gegrond, ontspannen en met vertrouwen arriveert. | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? This preparatory program is designed specifically for practitioners who want to test their readiness. | Why Choose YogaGarhi | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | De meeste scholen zwaaien studenten uit op de diploma-uitreiking. Wij niet. Gedurende 12 maanden na je opleiding krijg je maandelijkse live sessies met ons: feedback op je lessen als je lesgeeft, begeleiding voor je eigen sadhana als je niet lesgeeft, en een docent aan wie je al je vragen kunt stellen. Dit is geen marketingtruc: studenten uit eerdere lichtingen van onze oprichter oefenen na 3, 4 of zelfs 5 jaar nog steeds online met hem. We hebben simpelweg een belofte gemaakt van wat altijd al vanzelfsprekend was. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |

| 100hr Bali YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Onze 200 uur yogadocentenopleiding op Bali is een grondig basisprogramma voor wie zelfverzekerd yogales wil geven of yoga diep wil integreren in het eigen leven. Tijdens de opleiding beoefen je dagelijks asana's, verdiep je je in uitlijning en bestudeer je pranayama, meditatie, filosofie en didactiek. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. | 300hr Bali Advanced YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Onze 200 uur yogadocentenopleiding op Bali is een grondig basisprogramma voor wie zelfverzekerd yogales wil geven of yoga diep wil integreren in het eigen leven. YogaGarhi bevindt zich in Gianyar, op circa 15 km van Ubud, te midden van rustgevende natuur. Tijdens de opleiding beoefen je dagelijks asana's, verdiep je je in uitlijning en bestudeer je pranayama, meditatie, filosofie en didactiek. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. | 100hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Onze 200 uur yogadocentenopleiding op Bali is een grondig basisprogramma voor wie zelfverzekerd yogales wil geven of yoga diep wil integreren in het eigen leven. YogaGarhi bevindt zich in Gianyar, op circa 15 km van Ubud, te midden van rustgevende natuur. Tijdens de opleiding beoefen je dagelijks asana's, verdiep je je in uitlijning en bestudeer je pranayama, meditatie, filosofie en didactiek. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. | 200hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Onze 200 uur yogadocentenopleiding op Bali is een grondig basisprogramma voor wie zelfverzekerd yogales wil geven of yoga diep wil integreren in het eigen leven. YogaGarhi bevindt zich in Gianyar, op circa 15 km van Ubud, te midden van rustgevende natuur. Tijdens de opleiding beoefen je dagelijks asana's, verdiep je je in uitlijning en bestudeer je pranayama, meditatie, filosofie en didactiek. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. | teachers | Born and raised in the sacred land of Devprayag India, where Mother Ganga takes her first divine form, Sachin Ji's journey into yoga was not a choice but a calling. Growing up amidst rivers, temples, and the quiet discipline of a yogic lifestyle, yoga became woven into his breath, his thoughts, and his way of living. | Geboren en getogen in het heilige Devprayag in India, waar Moeder Ganga haar goddelijke vorm aanneemt, was de reis van Sachin Ji naar yoga geen keuze maar een roeping.
